# Unreal Engine 5.8 documentation map

How to use: search this file (Ctrl+F / grep) for the topic, open the **[core]** page first (every link already carries `?application_version=5.8`; keep that query string if you edit a URL), then [useful] pages for depth. [skip] marks pages a small single-player jam game should not need. Prefer the kit notes in `docs/ue/*.md` when one exists for the system, since those are verified against 5.8.4 headers and record gotchas; use this map to find the official page behind them. Within each section entries are ordered core, then useful, then skip. API reference is not crawled: use the roots below.

Crawled 2026-10-24 from `unreal-engine-5-8-documentation` (breadth-first, depth up to 4, 1500-page cap reached). 1500 pages crawled (11 empty/not-found shells dropped): 199 core, 860 useful, 197 skip listed individually, 233 more folded into the skipped-area hubs at the bottom.

API roots: [C++ API](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/API?application_version=5.8) | [Blueprint API](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/BlueprintAPI?application_version=5.8) | [Python API](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/python-api/?application_version=5.8)

Sections: Understanding the Basics | Working with Content | Building Virtual Worlds | Designing Visuals, Rendering & Lighting | Creating Visual Effects | Blueprints Visual Scripting | Programming with C++ | Editor Scripting & Automation | Gameplay Framework & Systems | Input | Physics, Collision & Destruction | AI | Gameplay Ability System | User Interfaces | Audio | Animating Characters and Objects | Cinematics | Testing, Profiling & Optimizing | Sharing and Releasing | Setting Up Your Production Pipeline | Samples, Templates & Tutorials | AI / MCP / LLM tooling | What's New, Beta & Experimental | Skipped areas

## Understanding the Basics (editor, actors, projects, assets)

- **[core]** [Understanding the Basics of Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/understanding-the-basics-of-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Essential skills and concepts to help you get started in Unreal Engine.
- **[core]** [Actors and Geometry in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/actors-and-geometry-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Defines Actors and describes how to use them in level design. Also includes a rundown of the most common types of Actors.
- **[core]** [Content Browser in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/content-browser-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - A tool you can use to view, manage, and work with all of the Assets in your project.
- **[core]** [Creating a New Project in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/creating-a-new-project-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Describes how to create and configure a new project in Unreal Engine.
- **[core]** [Placing Actors in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/placing-actors-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Shows how you can place Actors such as props, lights, and cameras into your Level.
- **[core]** [Playing and Simulating in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/playing-and-simulating-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Play-testing and simulating your game inside the Unreal Editor.
- **[core]** [Transforming Actors in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - How to modify the location, rotation, and scale of Actors in a Level.
- **[core]** [Units of Measurement in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/units-of-measurement-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Measure quantities of interest in Unreal Engine.

## Blueprints Visual Scripting

- **[core]** [Blueprints Visual Scripting in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/blueprints-visual-scripting-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Overview of using the Blueprint visual scripting system for gameplay.
- **[core]** [Blueprint Best Practices in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/blueprint-best-practices-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Tips and tricks to help you make decisions about when to use Blueprints and how best to set them up.
- **[core]** [Blueprint Communication Usage in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/blueprint-communication-usage-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Overview of when to use different methods of Blueprint Communications.
- **[core]** [Blueprint Debugger in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/blueprint-debugger-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Pause Blueprint execution using Breakpoints to inspect graphs and the values of their variables in Unreal Engine.
- **[core]** [Blueprint Interface in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/blueprint-interface-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Blueprints that declare functions to define an interface between Blueprints.
- **[core]** [Event Dispatchers in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/event-dispatchers-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Allows a Blueprint Class to report on its state to the Level Blueprint.
- **[core]** [Introduction to Blueprints Visual Scripting in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/introduction-to-blueprints-visual-scripting-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Introduction to visual scripting with Blueprints in Unreal Engine.
- **[core]** [Blueprint Variables in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/blueprint-variables-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Properties that hold a value or reference an Object or Actor in the world.
- **[core]** [Functions in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/functions-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Node graphs that can be executed, or called, from another graph.
- **[core]** [Actor Communication in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/actor-communication-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Overview of the different methods of Actor Communication.
- **[core]** [Blueprint Function Libraries in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/blueprint-function-libraries-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Information about Blueprint Function Libraries for C++ in Unreal Engine.

- **[core]** [Casting Quick Start Guide in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/casting-quick-start-guide-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Quick Start for the Casting communication method.

## Animating Characters and Objects

- **[core]** [Animating Characters and Objects in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/animating-characters-and-objects-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Explore Unreal Engine's animation tools and editors for working with 2D and 3D characters and objects.
- **[core]** [Animation Blueprints in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/animation-blueprints-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Animation Blueprints are visual scripts that are used for the creation and control of complex animation behaviors.
- **[core]** [Skeletal Mesh Animation System in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/skeletal-mesh-animation-system-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Unreal Engine's system for animation and controlling characters.

- **[core]** [State Machines in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/state-machines-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Create logic-based branching animation by using State Machines.
- **[core]** [Animation Montage in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/animation-montage-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Animation Montages are animation assets that enable you to combine animations in a single asset and control playback using Blueprints.
- **[core]** [Animation Sequences in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/animation-sequences-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Animation assets that contains animations for skeletal meshes.
- **[core]** [Blend Spaces in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/blend-spaces-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Blend Spaces are graphs where you can plot any number of animations to be blended between based on the values of multiple inputs.

## Cinematics (Sequencer, Movie Render Queue)

- [useful] [Cinematics and Movie Making in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/cinematics-and-movie-making-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Sequencer is Unreal Engine's multi-track editor used for creating and previewing cinematic sequences in real time.
- [useful] [Rendering High Quality Frames with Movie Render Queue in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/rendering-high-quality-frames-with-movie-render-queue-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - A how-to guide for configuring the Unreal Engine Movie Render Queue feature to get high-quality cinematics—particularly when using ray tracing.
- [useful] [Animation Curve Editor in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/animation-curve-editor-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Tweak your keyframes and curves by using the Curve Editor and tools within it.

- [useful] [Blending Animation Blueprints with Sequencer in Unreal Engine](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/blending-animation-blueprints-with-sequencer-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.8) - Describes how you can take a the pose from an Animation Blueprint and blend it with animations defined inside a Level Sequence.
